Let's try piecewise linear or more steps with values forming a design that equalizes convolution. This is like minimizing L∞ norm of square of polynomial of piecewise constant. We can optimize continuous piecewise constant with K small. Discretize into n equal intervals with K? Actually we can directly optimize sequence with block values: K blocks each of length L=n/K (or variable). The convolution of block-constant vector has coefficients that are piecewise linear/quadratic. We can set up nonlinear optimization over K block heights. This is much lower-dimensional and can approximate continuous optimum. For n large (e.g., n=1000) and K=10-30, use scipy minimize or custom gradient-free. This likely finds score <1.5. Great! We don't need n=3809; can choose n maybe a few thousand for discretization of continuous density. Scores will approach continuous 2C. Need continuous C below .751. More blocks can approach. We can numerically optimize K block heights. Then return length n = K*L. Evaluator will compute discrete convolution; if block lengths >1, within-block convolution adds triangular bumps that may increase max. For large L, within-block coefficient from two adjacent blocks: if blocks i,j constant A_i, A_j, their cross contribution to lag k is A_i A_j times number of pairs (p,q) with p+q = k within blocks. For large L, for interior lags, count ≈ L (not L^2?) Let's derive: Each block length L. Convolution of two length-L constant blocks with heights a,b. If non-adjacent, cross full overlap for L lags has value L a b (not L^2? Sum over L pairs a b each = L a b). The total sequence sum = L * S where S=sum block heights. Original discrete score: 2n max_b / (L S)^2. If n=K L, max_b ≈ L * max_{i,j,l} etc plus within block L a_i^2. This is ≍ L. Score ≈2 K L * L * M_block / (L^2 S^2)=2K * M_block/S^2. Here if we define continuous p_i = L * a_i? Hmm. Let's map: Let block heights h_i (sum H). Normal full sequence a = h_i repeated L times. Sum total = L H. b_k coefficients: For lags within combined block convolution, roughly L * c_k where c is convolution of sequence h (but with block scale? Actually convolution of repeated blocks: (J_L * J_L) has triangular value up to L; cross of block i and j constant h_i h_j times overlap length. For interior overlap, length L. So b ≈ L * (h*h) plus boundary triangular within each block also L*h_i^2. More exactly b = L * (h*h) plus edge corrections O(h_i^2). For large L, dominated by L h*h. Then score =2KL * [L * max(h*h)] / (L^2 H^2) =2K * max(h*h)/H^2. But note h length K; the score for h as a sequence of length K would be 2K max(h*h)/H^2 (with sum H). Optimization loop:
For a candidate vector a normalized sum1:
Evaluate score. If < best, store.
For each candidate, run a local improvement function `refine(a, budget)` that alternates:
1. LP direction with mixing small tau (e.g., 0.1) if LP feasible.
2. Coordinate descent steps (random coordinate multiplier) with exact eval.
3. Occasional random restart/mutation.
But we can combine globally: keep a population of candidates (length maybe fixed n). For each generation, pick best, apply improvement. Since objective depends on n, global best across n.

Let's design `local_search(a, budget)` for given length N:
```
def score(a):
   a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return inf
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   return 2*len(a)*b.max()/s/s
```
Use exact `evaluate_sequence` for acceptance? For internal speed, we can compute score ourselves; final best verify with evaluate_sequence. The evaluator does same (clips). We'll use internal for speed.

`normalize(a)`: a = np.maximum(a,0)/a.sum().

Coordinate descent:
```
def local_search(a, time_limit):
    a = normalize(a.copy())
    best = a; best_sc = score(a); rng=...
    n=a.size; width=min(n, max(1,n//16))
    for it in range(...):
       if time...break
       # choose move type
       if rng.random()<0.5: single coordinate
       else: block
       try multipliers from [0.7,0.85,1.0? no,1.2,1.5] and maybe if no improve try smaller
       # because objective invariant, we can modify without normalizing? normalize after candidate.
       base=a.copy()
       improved=False
       for mult in [0.8,1.25,0.6,1.6,0.9,1.1]:
           cand=base.copy()
           cand[idx] *= mult
           cand=normalize(cand)
           sc=score(cand)
           if sc<best_sc:
                best=a=cand; best_sc=sc; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
           # maybe backtrack? remain
    return best,best_sc
```

def _score_np(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    return float(2*a.size*b.max()/(s*s))
```

Coordinate hill climbing function with time limit and max_steps:
```
def _coord_hill(a, deadline, target=...):
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000).astype(float).copy()
    s=a.sum()
    if s==0: return...
    a/=s
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    score = 2*n*b.max()  # since s=1
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(...)
    n=a.size
    # precompute multipliers
    for step in range(max_steps):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       # occasionally random block full
       i=rng.integers(0,n)
       old=a[i]
       if old==0: multipliers maybe [1e-3,1e-2,1/n]
       else: multipliers = [old*r for r in ratios]
       best_trial=None...
       for m in ratios:
          new=old*m
          delta=new-old
          # candidate b copy? We'll compute max via two parts
          cand=b.copy()
          if delta:
             cand[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a  # Note a old; if i segment includes old; okay because cross term uses old a excluding? The formula uses old a; if a[i] in segment, okay. For k=2i, 2δ old_i plus δ².
             if 2*i < len(b): cand[2*i] += delta*delta
          cand_s = s + (new-old) # or s if we don't normalize? We can keep sum variable.
          sc = 2*n*cand.max()/(cand_s*cand_s)
          if sc < score:
             accept...
```
But if we accept, b update needs candidate b; we have cand. sum += delta. a[i]=new. score=sc. best update.
If no accept, do nothing. To allow larger moves when stuck, we can also try adding a small value to zero coordinates, or scaling a random block.

This hill climbing only accepts improvements. It may stall. Add "full random restarts" from new initial sequences. Also add "annealing" occasionally: if no improvement for L steps, accept a random perturbation even if worse (up to a threshold) to escape. Keep best. Internal score nonmonotonic okay.
Annealing:
- step ratio T decreases. If no improvement count > e.g., 100*n? Then choose a random coordinate or block, generate perturbation, compute score; if improvement accept; else if rng.random() < exp((best_score - cand_score)/T) and cand_score < 10*best_score, accept.
But computing exp with score differences; objective scale. Use temperature in score units (e.g., 0.1 to 0.01).
However, accepting worse can increase score but may escape.

We can implement a custom gradient descent on z:
```
def softobj_grad(z, beta):
   a = exp(z-zmax); a/=a.sum()
   b=np.convolve(a,a)
   mb=b.max()
   w=np.exp(beta*(b-mb))/sum(...)
   u=np.convolve(w[::-1], a)[2*n-2:3*n-2]
   s = u - (a*u).sum()
   grad = a*s
   # F = (mb + log(Σexp(β(b-mb)))/β)
   return F, grad
```
Then line search: z_new = z - lr*grad; evaluate true score (or F) and accept if improved. We can use backtracking. Use multiple initial z and betas. Since objective scale invariant with softmax normalized, true score =2n*max(b). We can use true score for acceptance. Gradient may go to zeros; z range can drift; we can renormalize z by subtract mean, clip [-20,20] maybe.

def run_gradient(z0, deadline, slice_fraction):
    z=copy; clip
    a=softmax; score
    if score<best: update
    for beta in [n/10, n/5, n/2]:
       st=0
       while time < deadline and st<2000:
          F, grad = compute(z,beta)
          if all grad tiny: break
          improved=False
          for step in [0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625,0.078125?]:
             zt=clip(z - step*grad)
             sc = true_score(softmax(zt))
             if sc < score - 1e-12:
                 z=zt; score=sc; accept update global; improved=True; st=0; break
          if not improved:
             st+=1
             # maybe increase beta? move start point
             # optional add small random z noise to escape
             if st%50==0: z += rng.normal(0, 0.1, n); z=clip(z); score=...
             if st>200: break
    # coordinate descent final on z
```

Detailed algorithm:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1500):
    t0=monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-10
    rng=seed
    best=None; best_score=inf
    def consider(a):
       nonlocal best,best_score
       a=np.clip(np.asarray(a,float),0,1000)
       if a.size==0 or not finite: return
       sc=evaluate_sequence(a.tolist())
       if sc < best_score:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
    # evaluate parent if available (for fallback)
    if 'height_sequence_1' in globals(): consider(height_sequence_1)
    # define score_np, softmax, grad
    for n in [32,64,128,256,512]:
       if time > deadline-30: break
       time_per_n = allocate based on n? e.g., slice = remaining * weights/(sum remaining)
       for start_idx in range(max_starts):
          if time...:break
          z=initial_z(n,rng,start_idx)
          improve_gradient_z(z, n, deadline_start_slice)
          consider(a)
          # coordinate refine on a if time
    # If no best or best_score inf, return [1.0]
    # final check best with evaluate, return list
```

Gradient improvement for a given n and z:
```
def gradient_improve(z, n, deadline):
   z = np.clip(z, -30,30)-z.mean()
   a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a)
   a_best=a.copy()
   for beta in betas:
      no_imp=0
      for it in range(iter_max):
         if time>deadline: break
         b=conv(a,a); mb=b.max(); w=softmax(beta*(b-mb))  # sum weights
         u=conv(w[::-1],a)[2*n-2:3*n-2]
         s=u - a.dot(u)
         grad=n*n*a*s
         if np.max(np.abs(grad))<1e-8: break
         # step trial sizes descending from 0.5 maybe
         improved=False
         for st in [0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.5]: # include 0.5 maybe
             zt=z-st*grad
             zt=np.clip(zt - zt.mean(), -30,30)
             at=softmax(zt)
             if at.sum()<=0: continue
             sc_t=score_np(at)
             if sc_t < score - 1e-12:
                 z=zt; a=at; score=sc_t; improved=True; break
         if improved: no_imp=0
         else:
             no_imp+=1
             if no_imp in [10,30,100]:
                 # random small perturbation
                 z += rng.normal(0, 0.1, n)
                 z=...
                 a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a)
             if no_imp>200: break
   return a, score
```

```
def coord_refine(a, deadline):
   a=a.copy(); s=a.sum(); a/=s; b=conv(a,a); score= score_np(a)
   # Use incremental moves for single coordinate, with occasional full block.
   max_steps based on n: 2e6 // n maybe?
   for step in range(max_steps):
      if time>deadline: break
      i=rng.integers(0,n)
      old=a[i]
      # ratios: if old==0 use small vals; else multiplicative
      ratios=[0.5,0.8,1.25,2.0,0.9,1.1]
      best_new=None; best_sc=score
      # To avoid copying b 6 times, we can compute candidate max using numpy slice, but okay.
      for r in ratios:
         new=old*r
         delta=new-old
         if delta==0 continue
         cand=b.copy()
         cand[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a
         if 2*i < 2*n-1: cand[2*i] += delta*delta
         sc=2*n*cand.max()  # since sum normalized? If old sum=1, new sum changes; score uses sum. We can either not normalize and use new_sum=cand? Score scale invariant but after multiplicative, sum changes. The b candidate corresponds to a_new with sum 1+delta (if old sum=1). Score =2n max / (1+delta)^2. So include.
      if accepted: update a,b,s? If s not 1, scale? We can maintain a not normalized? Start normalized sum1. If accept, sum becomes 1+delta. To prevent drift, normalize a,b to sum1 after acceptance: a/=s; b/=s*s; s=1. Score unchanged. Good.
```

def downsample_to(arr, n):
   m=len(arr)
   idx=np.linspace(0,m,n+1).astype(int)
   out=np.array([arr[idx[i]:idx[i+1]].mean()+1e-9 for i in range(n)])
   return np.log(out)
```

Coordinate full score uses exact incremental. For n=512, can run many steps. Let's implement `coordinate_refine(a, deadline, rng)`:
```
def coord_refine(a, deadline, rng):
    a=np.maximum(a,0).astype(float).copy()
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return a, inf
    a/=s; b=np.convolve(a,a); score=2*n*b.max()
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    step=0
    max_steps = max(1000, 20_000_000 // (n*n))? Not, each step O(n). Use time.
    stall=0
    while time<deadline:
       if step % 2000==0 and all... break? no.
       # coordinate move with adaptive ratios
       i=rng.integers(0,n)
       old=a[i]
       # ratios based on old: if old very small, try set to mean*exp noise
       if old < 1e-8:
          candidates=[1/(n*10), 1/n, 10/n]
       else:
          candidates=[old*r for r in [0.5,0.8,1.25,2.0,0.9,1.1,0.7,1.4]]
       best_cand=None
       for val in candidates:
          if val<0: continue
          delta=val-old
          if delta==0: continue
          cand=b.copy()
          if delta:
             cand[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a
             ip=2*i
             if ip < len(b): cand[ip] += delta*delta
          new_sum=1+delta
          if new_sum <= 1e-12: continue
          sc=2*n*cand.max()/(new_sum*new_sum)
          if sc < score - 1e-12:
             best_cand=(val,cand,sc); break # accept first improving? maybe best among all to avoid small. We can evaluate all and take best.
       if best_cand:
          val,cand,sc=best_cand
          a[i]=val; b=cand; score=sc
          # renormalize
          s=1+val-old
          if abs(s-1)>1e-6:
              a/=s; b/=s*s; score=2*n*b.max() # should same
          best=...; stall=0
       else:
          stall+=1
       step+=1
       if stall>1000:
          # random block perturbation
          width=min(n, max(1,n//10))
          start=rng.integers(0,n-width)
          a[start:start+width] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.3,width))
          a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); a/=s; b=np.convolve(a,a); score=2*n*b.max()
          if score<best_score: best...
          stall=0
    return best,best_score
```
Taking first improving ratio may be slow; evaluate all ratios and choose best to make larger moves. For 8 ratios, b.copy 8 times. n=512, 8*512*100k=409M; okay maybe. We can choose fewer [0.8,1.25,0.6,1.6] for speed, and when no accept try smaller [0.9,1.1].

We can also compute improved by using `cand = b + addition` but b copy still. For small n okay.

Softmax:
```
def softmax(z):
    z = z - np.max(z)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite: ones
    return e/s
```
If z clipped to [-30,30], sum finite.

a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-12: return inf

```

`improve_z`:
```
def improve_z(z, n, deadline):
    z = np.asarray(z,float).copy(); z = z - z.mean(); z=np.clip(z,-30,30)
    a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
    max_iters = int(max(50, 5000000 / (n*n)))? For n=512: 19; too low. Maybe fixed by phase time.
    betas=[max(1,n//20), max(2,n//8), max(4,n//4), max(8,n//2)]
    for beta in betas:
       no_improve=0
       while time.monotonic()<deadline and no_improve<200:
          b=np.convolve(a,a); mb=b.max()
          # if score already good? break
          w=...; u=...; s=...; grad=n*n*a*s
          gmax=np.max(np.abs(grad))
          if not finite or gmax<1e-9: break
          improved=False
          for step in [0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.4,0.005]:
             zt=z - step*grad
             zt=zt-zt.mean(); zt=np.clip(zt,-30,30)
             at=softmax(zt)
             sc=score_np(at)
             if sc < score - 1e-12:
                z=zt; a=at; score=sc; improved=True
                if score<best_score: best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score
                no_improve=0; break
          if not improved:
             no_improve += 1
             if no_improve == 30 or 80 or 150:
                z += rng.normal(0, 0.2, n); z=... a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a)
                # if worse, still continue
             if no_improve>200: break
    return best_a,best_score
```
When no improvement, the current score may be best for that z; add perturbation to escape. But after perturbation, score may be worse; we should not compare current score? We accept perturbation and continue, but best_a stores best. Good.
The line search order includes 0.2 first; if too large fails, smaller. Maybe include 0.05 before 0.2? If gradient direction good, 0.2 improves; if not, 0.02. Order [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.2,0.01,0.4].

Coordinate refine uses exact score; can be called on best_a.
```
def refine_coord(a, deadline, rng):
   a=...; s=a.sum(); if s<=0 return...
   a=a/s; b=np.convolve(a,a); score=2*n*b.max(); best=a.copy(); bestscore=score
   ratios = [0.6,0.85,1.2,1.7,0.95,1.05] maybe.
   stall=0
   while time<deadline:
      i=rng.integers(0,n)
      old=a[i]
      if old < 1e-8:
          vals=[a.mean()*r for r in [0.1,0.5,2] if ...]
      else:
          vals=[old*r for r in ratios]
      cand_best=None
      for val in vals:
         if val<=0: continue
         delta=val-old
         if delta==0: continue
         temp=b.copy()
         temp[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a
         p=i*2
         if p < len(temp): temp[p]+=delta*delta
         ns=1+delta
         if ns<=0: continue
         sc=2*n*temp.max()/(ns*ns)
         if sc < score - 1e-12 and (cand_best is None or sc<cand_best[2]):
             cand_best=(val,temp,sc)
      if cand_best:
         val,temp,sc=cand_best
         a[i]=val; b=temp; score=sc
         s=1+val-old
         if abs(s-1)>1e-8:
            a/=s; b/=s*s; score=2*n*b.max()
         if score<bestscore: best=a.copy(); bestscore=score
         stall=0
      else:
         stall+=1
      if stall>500:
         # block noise
         w=max(3,n//16)
         st=rng.integers(0,n-w+1)
         a[st:st+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.2,w))
         a=np.clip(a,0,1e3); s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
         b=np.convolve(a,a); score=2*n*b.max()
         if score<bestscore: best=a.copy(); bestscore=score
         stall=0
   return best,bestscore
```
Note if old=0 and val set, after renormalization many values slightly changed; temp formula b+2δa+δ² is correct for a_new before renormalization with current a sum1. Good.
When accepting best candidate among vals, we compute each temp copy. Could be many copies. Limit n.

Smoothing function:
```
def smooth_to(a, m):
   n=len(a)
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   y=a/a.sum() # heights
   xm=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
   ym=np.interp(xm, x, y, left=y[0], right=y[-1])
   # maybe also apply a small noise? no.
   ym=np.clip(ym,0,1000); s=ym.sum(); return ym/s
```
This is linear density shape. Good.

Let's implement `consider`:
```
def consider(arr):
   nonlocal best, best_score
   if arr is None: return
   arr=np.clip(np.asarray(arr,float),0,1000)
   if arr.size==0 or not np.isfinite(arr).all(): return
   sc=float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
       best=arr.copy(); best_score=sc
```

def optimize_n(n, trials, phase_deadline):
    if n<2: return
    phase_len = max(0, phase_deadline - time.monotonic())
    per = max(3.0, 0.8*phase_len/trials)
    for trial in range(trials):
       t=time.monotonic()
       if t > phase_deadline - 2: break
       z = make_initial(n, trial, rng)
       g_deadline = min(phase_deadline, t + per*0.65)
       a,_ = improve_z(z,n,g_deadline,rng)
       consider(a)
       rem_deadline = min(phase_deadline, t + per*0.95)
       if time.monotonic() < rem_deadline - 2 and best is not None:
           a2,_ = coord_refine(a, rem_deadline, rng)
           consider(a2)
```

def de_search(n, budget):
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
    def obj(z):
        a=softmax(z); return score_np(a)

    # Use int(max(20, 60//n?)? popsize 12, maxiter 200, tol 1e-3, seed, polish=True

```

`score_np`:
```
    def score_np(a):
        a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
        s=float(a.sum())
        if s<=1e-12: return np.inf
        b=np.convolve(a,a)
        return float(2*a.size*float(b.max())/(s*s))
```
For small a sum >0. If a contains nan, return inf.

`softmax`:
```
    def softmax(z):
       z=np.asarray(z,float)
       z=z-np.max(z)
       e=np.exp(z)
       s=e.sum()
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
          return np.ones(len(z))/len(z)
       return e/s
```

```
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if idx==0: return (x-.5)*2
    ...
    if parent is not None and len(parent)>=n:
       # downsample using block averages
```

    def improve_z(z,n,stop,rng_local):
       z=... 
       a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a)
       if not finite: a=np.ones(n)/n; score=2.0; z=zeros
       best=a.copy(); bscore=score
       bets=[max(1,n//20), max(2,n//8), max(5,n//4), max(10,n//2)]
       for beta in bets:
          no=0
          while time.monotonic()<stop and no<200:
             b=np.convolve(a,a); mb=b.max()
             if mb<=0: break
             ex=np.exp(beta*(b-mb)); ws=ex.sum()
             if ws>0: w=ex/ws else w...
             u=np.convolve(w[::-1], a)[2*n-2:n-2:-1]
             s=u - float(np.dot(a,u))
             grad=(float(n)*float(n))*a*s
             if not finite... break
             improved=False
             for step in (0.1,0.05,0.02,0.2,0.01,0.4):
                zt=z-step*grad
                zt=zt-zt.mean(); zt=np.clip(zt,-30,30)
                at=softmax(zt)
                sc=score_np(at)
                if sc < score - 1e-12:
                    ...
             if improved: no=0
             else:
                no+=1
                if no in (40,100,160):
                    z += rng_local.normal(0,0.25,n)
                    z = z - z.mean(); z = np.clip(z,-30,30)
                    a=softmax(z); score=score_np(a)
                if no>=200: break
       return best, bscore
```

`coord_refine`: Use `stop` time. Need handle n large. If `stop <= time`: return.
```
    def coord_refine(a, stop):
       a=np.asarray(a,float).copy()
       if a.size==0: return a, np.inf
       a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum()
       if s<=0: return a,np.inf
       a/=s; n=a.size; b=np.convolve(a,a); score=float(2*n*b.max())
       best=a.copy(); bscore=score
       stall=0
       while time.monotonic()<stop:
          i=int(rng.integers(0,n))
          old=a[i]
          meanv=a.mean()
          if old <= 1e-10:
              vals=[meanv*r for r in (0.1,0.5,2.0)]
          else:
              vals=[old*r for r in (0.55,0.8,1.25,1.8,0.9,1.1)]
          bestval=None; besttemp=None; bestsc=score
          for val in vals:
             if val <=0: continue
             delta=val-old
             if abs(delta) < 1e-15: continue
             temp=b.copy()
             temp[i:i+n] += 2.0*delta*a
             p=2*i
             if p < len(temp): temp[p] += delta*delta
             ns=1.0+delta
             if ns <= 1e-12: continue
             sc=float(2*n*temp.max())/(ns*ns)
             if sc < bestsc - 1e-12:
                bestsc=sc; bestval=val; besttemp=temp
          if besttemp is not None:
             a[i]=bestval; b=besttemp; score=bestsc
             ns=1.0+ (bestval-old)
             if abs(ns-1)>1e-8:
                 a/=ns; b/= (ns*ns)
                 score=float(2*n*b.max())
             if score < bscore - 1e-12:
                 best=a.copy(); bscore=score
             stall=0
          else:
             stall += 1
          if stall > 800:
             w=max(3, n//16)
             st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
             a[st:st+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25,w))
             a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
             ss=a.sum()
             if ss>0: a/=ss
             b=np.convolve(a,a); score=float(2*n*b.max())
             if score<bscore: best=a.copy(); bscore=score
             stall=0
       return best, bscore
```
When `bestvalue` large and `ns` negative? val>0, old≥0, delta> -old so ns>0.

    def optimize_n(n, trials, phase_deadline):
       phase_len = max(0, phase_deadline - time.monotonic())
       per = max(3.0, 0.9*phase_len/trials)
       for job in range(trials):
          if time.monotonic() > phase_deadline - 2: break
          t=time.monotonic()
          z=make_initial(n, job)
          g_stop=min(phase_deadline, t+per*0.65)

          if best_score < 1.45: break
          c_stop=min(phase_deadline, t+per*0.97)

             a2,_=coord_refine(a,c_stop)
             consider(a2)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s)) - 15.0

    best = None
    best_score = np.inf
    parent = None

    try:
        parr = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parr.ndim == 1 and parr.size > 0 and np.isfinite(parr).all():
            parent = np.clip(parr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    def consider(arr):
        nonlocal best, best_score
        if arr is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if a.sum() <= 0.0:
            return
        sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = a.copy()
            best_score = sc

    if parent is not None:
        consider(parent)

    def score_np(a):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0:
            return np.inf
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return np.inf
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        if not np.isfinite(b).all():
            return np.inf
        return float(2.0 * a.size * float(b.max()) / (s * s))

    def softmax(z):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
        z = z - np.max(z)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(e.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
        return e / s

    def downsample_z(prov, n):
        m = prov.size
        idx = np.linspace(0, m, n + 1, dtype=int)
        vals = []
        for i in range(n):
            v = float(prov[idx[i]:idx[i + 1]].mean())
            vals.append(v + 1e-9)
        vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=np.float64)
        z = np.log(np.maximum(vals, 1e-12))
        return z - z.mean()

    def make_initial(n, job):
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        if job == 0:
            return (x - 0.5) * 2.0
        if job == 1:
            return (x - 0.5) * 4.0
        if job == 2:
            return (0.5 - x) * 4.0
        if job == 3:
            return -2.0 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)
        if job == 4:
            return np.where(x < 0.5, 1.0, 0.3)
        if job == 5:
            return np.where(x < 0.35, 1.0, -0.7)
        if job == 6:
            return 0.7 * np.cos(2 * np.pi * x) + 0.4 * np.cos(4 * np.pi * x + 1.0)
        if job == 7:
            z = np.zeros(n)
            for f, amp in ((1, 1.0), (2, 0.7), (3, 0.5), (5, 0.4), (8, 0.3)):
                z += amp * np.cos(2 * np.pi * f * x + rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            return z
        if job == 8:
            return rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        if job == 9:
            return rng.normal(0.0, 1.8, n)
        if job == 10 and parent is not None:
            return downsample_z(parent, n)
        return rng.normal(0.0, 1.2, n) - 0.3 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    def de_short(n, maxit):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 25:
            return

        def obj(z):
            return score_np(softmax(z))

        try:
            res = differential_evolution(
                obj,
                [(-12.0, 12.0)] * n,
                seed=int(seed) + n,
                maxiter=maxit,
                popsize=10,
                tol=1e-3,
                atol=1e-7,
                init="latinhypercube",
                polish=True,
            )
            if res.x is not None:
                consider(softmax(res.x))
        except Exception:
            pass

    def improve_z(z, n, stop):
        z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if z.size != n:
            z = np.zeros(n)
        z = z - z.mean()
        z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)

        a = softmax(z)
        score = score_np(a)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
            z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            score = 2.0
        out = a.copy()
        out_score = score

        for beta in (max(1.0, n / 20.0), max(2.0, n / 8.0), max(5.0, n / 4.0), max(10.0, n / 2.0)):
            no = 0
            while time.monotonic() < stop and no < 220:
                b = np.convolve(a, a)
                mb = float(b.max())
                if mb <= 1e-30:
                    break
                ex = np.exp(beta * (b - mb))
                ws = float(ex.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                    w = np.zeros_like(b)
                    w[mb == b] = 1.0
                    w /= w.sum()
                else:
                    w = ex / ws

                u = np.convolve(w[::-1], a)[2 * n - 2:n - 2:-1]
                s = u - float(np.dot(a, u))
                grad = (float(n) * float(n)) * a * s

                if not np.isfinite(grad).all() or np.max(np.abs(grad)) < 1e-12:
                    break

                improved = False
                for step in (0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.20, 0.01, 0.40):
                    zt = z - step * grad
                    zt = zt - zt.mean()
                    zt = np.clip(zt, -30.0, 30.0)
                    at = softmax(zt)
                    sc = score_np(at)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < score - 1e-12:
                        z = zt
                        a = at
                        score = sc
                        improved = True
                        if score < out_score - 1e-12:
                            out = a.copy()
                            out_score = score
                        break

                if not improved:
                    no += 1
                    if no in (40, 100, 170):
                        z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n)
                        z = z - z.mean()
                        z = np.clip(z, -30.0, 30.0)
                        a = softmax(z)
                        score = score_np(a)
                        if not np.isfinite(score):
                            a = out.copy()
                            z = np.zeros(n)
                            score = out_score
                    if no >= 220:
                        break
            if out_score < 1.10:
                break

        return out, out_score

    def coord_refine(a, stop):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if a.size == 0:
            return a, np.inf
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return a, np.inf
        a /= s
        n = a.size
        b = np.convolve(a, a)
        score = float(2 * n * b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return a, np.inf

        out = a.copy()
        out_score = score
        stall = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stop:
            i = int(rng.integers(0, n))
            old = float(a[i])
            mu = float(a.mean())

            if old <= 1e-10:
                vals = [mu * r for r in (0.1, 0.5, 2.0, 6.0)]
            else:
                vals = [old * r for r in (0.55, 0.80, 1.25, 1.80, 0.90, 1.10)]

            best_val = None
            best_sc = score
            best_temp = None
            for val in vals:
                if not np.isfinite(val) or val <= 0.0:
                    continue
                delta = val - old
                if abs(delta) < 1e-15:
                    continue
                temp = b.copy()
                temp[i:i + n] += 2.0 * delta * a
                p = 2 * i
                if p < temp.size:
                    temp[p] += delta * delta
                ns = 1.0 + delta
                if ns <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                sc = float(2 * n * temp.max()) / (ns * ns)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = sc
                    best_val = val
                    best_temp = temp

            if best_temp is not None:
                ns = 1.0 + (best_val - old)
                a[i] = best_val
                b = best_temp
                if abs(ns - 1.0) > 1e-9:
                    a /= ns
                    b /= (ns * ns)
                score = float(2 * n * b.max())
                if score < out_score - 1e-12:
                    out = a.copy()
                    out_score = score
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1

            if stall > 700:
                w = max(3, n // 16)
                st = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
                a[st:st + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, w))
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
                ss = float(a.sum())
                if ss > 1e-12:
                    a /= ss
                    b = np.convolve(a, a)
                    score = float(2 * n * b.max())
                    if score < out_score - 1e-12:
                        out = a.copy()
                        out_score = score
                stall = 0

        return out, out_score

    def smooth_to(a, m):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-12:
            return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / m
        a /= s
        n = a.size
        x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / n
        y = a
        xm = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) / m
        ym = np.interp(xm, x, y, left=y[0], right=y[-1])
        ym = np.clip(ym, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ym = np.maximum(ym, 1e-12)
        ym /= ym.sum()
        return ym

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 250:
        for nn, mx in ((8, 100), (12, 80), (16, 60)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 200:
                break
            de_short(nn, mx)
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 140:
            de_short(24, 45)

    if best_score > 1.48 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120:
        start_t = time.monotonic()
        total = max(1.0, deadline - start_t)
        plans = [
            (64, 4, 0.10),
            (128, 5, 0.20),
            (256, 6, 0.35),
            (512, 4, 0.35),
        ]
        cum = 0.0
        for n, tr, frac in plans:
            cum += frac
            phase_end = min(deadline, start_t + cum * total)
            if time.monotonic() > phase_end - 3 or best_score < 1.45:
                continue
            per_try = max(3.0, 0.85 * max(0.0, phase_end - time.monotonic()) / max(1, tr))
            for job in range(tr):
                if time.monotonic() > phase_end - 3:
                    break
                t_try = time.monotonic()
                if best_score < 1.45:
                    break
                z0 = make_initial(n, job)
                a, sc0 = improve_z(z0, n, min(phase_end, t_try + 0.60 * per_try))
                consider(a)
                c_stop = min(phase_end, t_try + 0.97 * per_try)
                if time.monotonic() < c_stop - 2:
                    a2, _ = coord_refine(a, c_stop)
                    consider(a2)

    if best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
        a_final, _ = coord_refine(best, deadline - 8)
        consider(a_final)

    if parent is not None and parent.size == 3809 and best is not None:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70:
            long_a = smooth_to(best, 3809)
            consider(long_a)
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45:
                long_a, _ = coord_refine(long_a, deadline - 40)
                consider(long_a)
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 100 and best_score > 1.48:
                zlong = np.log(np.maximum(long_a, 1e-300))
                zlong = zlong - zlong.mean()
                zlong = np.clip(zlong, -30.0, 30.0)
                long_g, _ = improve_z(zlong, 3809, min(deadline - 50, time.monotonic() + 80))
                consider(long_g)
                if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25:
                    long_c, _ = coord_refine(long_g, deadline - 20)
                    consider(long_c)

    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    final = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_score = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
